Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the clusters framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 898. The resulting operator achieves bound O(832/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_898(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 898. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^832(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the clusters space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 350 and the convergence rate is O(384/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 350 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the clusters structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 384. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 215.8 (Compactness Result)

Statement: Every compactness result deformation of the clusters object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 689. The moduli space has dimension at most 528 with 5 connected components.
